# PR #394 — Rebuild client UI in Dioxus from burncloud-ui

> **状态：** OPEN  
> **作者：** `rustburn`  
> **创建：** `2026-08-10T12:26:15Z`  
> **Base：** `main@956041a8b54d`  
> **Head：** `agent/dioxus-burncloud-ui-rebuild@8c3b7cb0128f`  
> **完整 E2E 拓扑基线：** `burncloud/burncloud@aa54e21393c6d46a6b09555ffd3661c1f22484f3`  
> **证据规则：** PR changed files / patch 为 `STATIC CONFIRMED`；下方完整 E2E 使用当前已审计 Atlas 拓扑，并在命中 PR changed file 的节点标记 `[PR CHANGED]`。旧 PR 的历史 head 可能与当前拓扑存在后续演化，因此未把当前拓扑伪装成历史源码逐行复现。

## PR 影响总览

- **Change Class：** `RUNTIME + UI`
- **Changed Files：** `224`
- **Additions / Deletions：** `+8034 / -19553`
- **受影响的完整 E2E Flow：** `62`
- **未映射 changed files：** `183`

**PR 说明摘要：** ## Summary - replace the legacy multi-crate BurnCloud client with a unified Dioxus client while preserving the `rustburn/burncloud-ui` information architecture and visual system - restore the real BurnCloud client/backend capabilities that were lost during the first UI-only rewrite - keep the existing BurnCloud server/router/database behavior as the source of truth rather than inventing prototype-only actions - preserve Windows Desktop frameless chrome, transparent title bar, system tray, LiveView integration, and web launch paths ## Functional migration The rebuilt console is no longer a static visual prototype. Protected console routes are behind a real JWT session gate and the active pages now call the current BurnCloud server APIs. ### Authentication / session - real `/api/auth/login` username/password authentication - real `/api/auth/register` account creation - real `/api/auth/forgot-password` recovery request - persist the returned JWT/current user for console API calls - protect console routes with `AuthGate` - show the real authenticated username/roles in the console shell - real Sign Out clears the JWT/local client session - passkey remains disabled because the current server exposes no passkey challenge endpoint ### Overview / Dashboard - live system metrics from `/console/api/monitor` - live channels/provider state - live user usage - live billing summary - latest r…

## 完整受影响 E2E Request Flow

### E2E FLOW #1 — GET /

**树路径：** `HTTP / API → Web UI / LiveView / WebSocket → GET /`  
**PR Impact：** `STATIC SOURCE-PATH MATCH`  
**本 Flow 命中的 changed files：** `crates/client/src/lib.rs`, `crates/client/src/app.rs`, `crates/client/crates/client-shared/src/i18n.rs`

```text
START
│
├─ 调用方与输入边界
│    ├─ Actor: User / SDK / Browser / Operator
│    ├─ Entry: GET /
│    ├─ Input sources
│    │    ├─ Method + URI path
│    │    ├─ Query string（如有）
│    │    ├─ HTTP headers
│    │    └─ Request body（如有）
│    └─ DECISION: TCP/HTTP 请求能否到达 BurnCloud listener?
│         ├─ NO  → 网络层失败；应用代码未执行 → END
│         └─ YES → 进入 Axum
│
▼
FILE: crates/server/src/lib.rs
│
├─ 统一 HTTP Server
│    ├─ start_server() 已在进程启动时完成
│    │    ├─ database 初始化
│    │    ├─ RouterDatabase::init()
│    │    ├─ UserDatabase::init()
│    │    ├─ create_app(...)
│    │    ├─ TcpListener::bind(...)
│    │    └─ axum::serve(listener, app)
│    ├─ 当前请求进入 Unified Axum App
│    └─ 全局 middleware
│         ├─ CORS
│         ├─ TraceLayer
│         ├─ SetRequestIdLayer
│         └─ PropagateRequestIdLayer
│
├─ 顶层 Route 决策
│    └─ DECISION: Unified App 是否已有显式/合并路由命中当前 Method + Path?
│         ├─ YES → merged LiveView/static route candidate
│         └─ NO → other route/fallback
│
├─ LiveView feature gate
│    └─ DECISION: enable_liveview == true?
│         ├─ NO → LiveView route unavailable; routing continues/falls back
│         └─ YES → match LiveView Router
│
▼
FILE: crates/client/src/lib.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│
├─ HTTP shell/static handler
│    ├─ route shell / preview / favicon according to path
│    └─ DECISION: requested LiveView/static route recognized?
│         ├─ NO → route miss
│         └─ YES → construct HTML/static response
│
▼
FILE: crates/client/src/app.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│
├─ Client route model
│    ├─ Dioxus Route enum represents browser-side view
│    ├─ App contexts: auth/theme/i18n/toast
│    └─ page-specific component selected after client session establishes
│
├─ Follow-up interactive transport
│    ├─ initial HTTP response delivers shell
│    └─ interactive events move to /ws LiveView session
│
│
├─ 源码函数展开（静态扫描确认）
│    ├─ FILE: crates/client/src/app.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    ├─ App()
│    │    │    └─ CALL → use_init_i18n() @ crates/client/crates/client-shared/src/i18n.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    ├─ launch_gui_with_tray()
│    └─ FILE: crates/client/crates/client-shared/src/i18n.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    ├─ use_init_i18n()
│
├─ 规则：只展开能够解析到 BurnCloud 仓库内部真实函数定义的调用；第三方库调用保留在主 E2E 中，不伪造源码目标文件
│

▼
END
     └─ Browser receives HTML/static shell; UI lifecycle continues separately
```

### E2E FLOW #2 — GET /home

**树路径：** `HTTP / API → Web UI / LiveView / WebSocket → GET /home`  
**PR Impact：** `STATIC SOURCE-PATH MATCH`  
**本 Flow 命中的 changed files：** `crates/client/src/lib.rs`, `crates/client/src/app.rs`, `crates/client/crates/client-shared/src/i18n.rs`

```text
START
│
├─ 调用方与输入边界
│    ├─ Actor: User / SDK / Browser / Operator
│    ├─ Entry: GET /home
│    ├─ Input sources
│    │    ├─ Method + URI path
│    │    ├─ Query string（如有）
│    │    ├─ HTTP headers
│    │    └─ Request body（如有）
│    └─ DECISION: TCP/HTTP 请求能否到达 BurnCloud listener?
│         ├─ NO  → 网络层失败；应用代码未执行 → END
│         └─ YES → 进入 Axum
│
▼
FILE: crates/server/src/lib.rs
│
├─ 统一 HTTP Server
│    ├─ start_server() 已在进程启动时完成
│    │    ├─ database 初始化
│    │    ├─ RouterDatabase::init()
│    │    ├─ UserDatabase::init()
│    │    ├─ create_app(...)
│    │    ├─ TcpListener::bind(...)
│    │    └─ axum::serve(listener, app)
│    ├─ 当前请求进入 Unified Axum App
│    └─ 全局 middleware
│         ├─ CORS
│         ├─ TraceLayer
│         ├─ SetRequestIdLayer
│         └─ PropagateRequestIdLayer
│
├─ 顶层 Route 决策
│    └─ DECISION: Unified App 是否已有显式/合并路由命中当前 Method + Path?
│         ├─ YES → merged LiveView/static route candidate
│         └─ NO → other route/fallback
│
├─ LiveView feature gate
│    └─ DECISION: enable_liveview == true?
│         ├─ NO → LiveView route unavailable; routing continues/falls back
│         └─ YES → match LiveView Router
│
▼
FILE: crates/client/src/lib.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│
├─ HTTP shell/static handler
│    ├─ route shell / preview / favicon according to path
│    └─ DECISION: requested LiveView/static route recognized?
│         ├─ NO → route miss
│         └─ YES → construct HTML/static response
│
▼
FILE: crates/client/src/app.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│
├─ Client route model
│    ├─ Dioxus Route enum represents browser-side view
│    ├─ App contexts: auth/theme/i18n/toast
│    └─ page-specific component selected after client session establishes
│
├─ Follow-up interactive transport
│    ├─ initial HTTP response delivers shell
│    └─ interactive events move to /ws LiveView session
│
│
├─ 源码函数展开（静态扫描确认）
│    ├─ FILE: crates/client/src/app.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    ├─ App()
│    │    │    └─ CALL → use_init_i18n() @ crates/client/crates/client-shared/src/i18n.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    ├─ launch_gui_with_tray()
│    └─ FILE: crates/client/crates/client-shared/src/i18n.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    ├─ use_init_i18n()
│
├─ 规则：只展开能够解析到 BurnCloud 仓库内部真实函数定义的调用；第三方库调用保留在主 E2E 中，不伪造源码目标文件
│

▼
END
     └─ Browser receives HTML/static shell; UI lifecycle continues separately
```

### E2E FLOW #6 — GET /reset-password

**树路径：** `HTTP / API → Web UI / LiveView / WebSocket → GET /reset-password`  
**PR Impact：** `STATIC SOURCE-PATH MATCH`  
**本 Flow 命中的 changed files：** `crates/client/src/lib.rs`, `crates/client/src/app.rs`, `crates/client/crates/client-shared/src/i18n.rs`

```text
START
│
├─ 调用方与输入边界
│    ├─ Actor: User / SDK / Browser / Operator
│    ├─ Entry: GET /reset-password
│    ├─ Input sources
│    │    ├─ Method + URI path
│    │    ├─ Query string（如有）
│    │    ├─ HTTP headers
│    │    └─ Request body（如有）
│    └─ DECISION: TCP/HTTP 请求能否到达 BurnCloud listener?
│         ├─ NO  → 网络层失败；应用代码未执行 → END
│         └─ YES → 进入 Axum
│
▼
FILE: crates/server/src/lib.rs
│
├─ 统一 HTTP Server
│    ├─ start_server() 已在进程启动时完成
│    │    ├─ database 初始化
│    │    ├─ RouterDatabase::init()
│    │    ├─ UserDatabase::init()
│    │    ├─ create_app(...)
│    │    ├─ TcpListener::bind(...)
│    │    └─ axum::serve(listener, app)
│    ├─ 当前请求进入 Unified Axum App
│    └─ 全局 middleware
│         ├─ CORS
│         ├─ TraceLayer
│         ├─ SetRequestIdLayer
│         └─ PropagateRequestIdLayer
│
├─ 顶层 Route 决策
│    └─ DECISION: Unified App 是否已有显式/合并路由命中当前 Method + Path?
│         ├─ YES → merged LiveView/static route candidate
│         └─ NO → other route/fallback
│
├─ LiveView feature gate
│    └─ DECISION: enable_liveview == true?
│         ├─ NO → LiveView route unavailable; routing continues/falls back
│         └─ YES → match LiveView Router
│
▼
FILE: crates/client/src/lib.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│
├─ HTTP shell/static handler
│    ├─ route shell / preview / favicon according to path
│    └─ DECISION: requested LiveView/static route recognized?
│         ├─ NO → route miss
│         └─ YES → construct HTML/static response
│
▼
FILE: crates/client/src/app.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│
├─ Client route model
│    ├─ Dioxus Route enum represents browser-side view
│    ├─ App contexts: auth/theme/i18n/toast
│    └─ page-specific component selected after client session establishes
│
├─ Follow-up interactive transport
│    ├─ initial HTTP response delivers shell
│    └─ interactive events move to /ws LiveView session
│
│
├─ 源码函数展开（静态扫描确认）
│    ├─ FILE: crates/client/src/app.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    ├─ App()
│    │    │    └─ CALL → use_init_i18n() @ crates/client/crates/client-shared/src/i18n.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    ├─ launch_gui_with_tray()
│    └─ FILE: crates/client/crates/client-shared/src/i18n.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    ├─ use_init_i18n()
│
├─ 规则：只展开能够解析到 BurnCloud 仓库内部真实函数定义的调用；第三方库调用保留在主 E2E 中，不伪造源码目标文件
│

▼
END
     └─ Browser receives HTML/static shell; UI lifecycle continues separately
```

### E2E FLOW #10 — GET /favicon.ico

**树路径：** `HTTP / API → Web UI / LiveView / WebSocket → GET /favicon.ico`  
**PR Impact：** `STATIC SOURCE-PATH MATCH`  
**本 Flow 命中的 changed files：** `crates/client/src/lib.rs`, `crates/client/src/app.rs`, `crates/client/crates/client-shared/src/i18n.rs`

```text
START
│
├─ 调用方与输入边界
│    ├─ Actor: User / SDK / Browser / Operator
│    ├─ Entry: GET /favicon.ico
│    ├─ Input sources
│    │    ├─ Method + URI path
│    │    ├─ Query string（如有）
│    │    ├─ HTTP headers
│    │    └─ Request body（如有）
│    └─ DECISION: TCP/HTTP 请求能否到达 BurnCloud listener?
│         ├─ NO  → 网络层失败；应用代码未执行 → END
│         └─ YES → 进入 Axum
│
▼
FILE: crates/server/src/lib.rs
│
├─ 统一 HTTP Server
│    ├─ start_server() 已在进程启动时完成
│    │    ├─ database 初始化
│    │    ├─ RouterDatabase::init()
│    │    ├─ UserDatabase::init()
│    │    ├─ create_app(...)
│    │    ├─ TcpListener::bind(...)
│    │    └─ axum::serve(listener, app)
│    ├─ 当前请求进入 Unified Axum App
│    └─ 全局 middleware
│         ├─ CORS
│         ├─ TraceLayer
│         ├─ SetRequestIdLayer
│         └─ PropagateRequestIdLayer
│
├─ 顶层 Route 决策
│    └─ DECISION: Unified App 是否已有显式/合并路由命中当前 Method + Path?
│         ├─ YES → merged LiveView/static route candidate
│         └─ NO → other route/fallback
│
├─ LiveView feature gate
│    └─ DECISION: enable_liveview == true?
│         ├─ NO → LiveView route unavailable; routing continues/falls back
│         └─ YES → match LiveView Router
│
▼
FILE: crates/client/src/lib.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│
├─ HTTP shell/static handler
│    ├─ route shell / preview / favicon according to path
│    └─ DECISION: requested LiveView/static route recognized?
│         ├─ NO → route miss
│         └─ YES → construct HTML/static response
│
▼
FILE: crates/client/src/app.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│
├─ Client route model
│    ├─ Dioxus Route enum represents browser-side view
│    ├─ App contexts: auth/theme/i18n/toast
│    └─ page-specific component selected after client session establishes
│
├─ Follow-up interactive transport
│    ├─ initial HTTP response delivers shell
│    └─ interactive events move to /ws LiveView session
│
│
├─ 源码函数展开（静态扫描确认）
│    ├─ FILE: crates/client/src/app.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    ├─ App()
│    │    │    └─ CALL → use_init_i18n() @ crates/client/crates/client-shared/src/i18n.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    ├─ launch_gui_with_tray()
│    └─ FILE: crates/client/crates/client-shared/src/i18n.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    ├─ use_init_i18n()
│
├─ 规则：只展开能够解析到 BurnCloud 仓库内部真实函数定义的调用；第三方库调用保留在主 E2E 中，不伪造源码目标文件
│

▼
END
     └─ Browser receives HTML/static shell; UI lifecycle continues separately
```

**树路径：** `HTTP / API → Web UI / LiveView / WebSocket → GET /preview/console/`

**本 Flow 命中的 changed files：** `crates/client/src/lib.rs`, `crates/client/src/app.rs`, `crates/client/crates/client-shared/src/i18n.rs`

```text
START
│
├─ 调用方与输入边界

│    │    ├─ Query string（如有）

│         └─ YES → match LiveView Router
│
```

### E2E FLOW #17 — burncloud

**树路径：** `CLI / Executables → burncloud → burncloud`  
**PR Impact：** `STATIC SOURCE-PATH MATCH`  
**本 Flow 命中的 changed files：** `crates/client/src/app.rs`

```text
START
│
├─ Shell / OS: burncloud（无参数）
│
▼
FILE: src/main.rs
│
├─ main()
│    ├─ dotenvy::dotenv()
│    ├─ ensure_master_key()
│    ├─ init_logging()
│    └─ match args == [binary]
│
├─ DECISION: target_os == windows?
│    ├─ YES
│    │    ├─ std::thread::spawn(background server thread)
│    │    │    ├─ tokio::runtime::Runtime::new()
│    │    │    ├─ HOST / PORT
│    │    │    └─ burncloud_server::start_server(host, port, false)
│    │    │         └─ enable_liveview=false：后台 Server 不挂 LiveView Router
│    │    └─ main thread → burncloud_client::launch_gui_with_tray()
│    │
│    └─ NO（Linux/macOS 等）
│         ├─ print Headless Mode startup line
│         └─ run_async_server()
│              └─ burncloud_server::start_server(host, port, true)
│
├─ Windows server thread 后续主链与 `burncloud server` 的 start_server/create_app/create_router_app 相同
├─ Non-Windows 后续主链与 `burncloud server` 完全相同
│
▼
FILE: crates/client/src/app.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│
├─ Windows GUI branch: launch_gui_with_tray()
│    ├─ WindowBuilder
│    ├─ Config::new().with_window(...).with_data_directory(...)
│    └─ LaunchBuilder::desktop().launch(AppWithTray)
│
├─ AppWithTray()
│    ├─ DesktopMode context
│    ├─ maximize window
│    ├─ std::thread::spawn(start_tray)
│    ├─ spawn async show-window poll loop
│    └─ render App()
│
└─ App()
```

```text
START
│

│    ├─ PriceCache::load(&db).await
│    │    └─ DECISION: load fails? → PriceCache::empty()
│    ├─ CostCalculator::new(price_cache.clone())
│    ├─ ExchangeRateService::new(db.clone())
│    ├─ load_rates_from_db().await
│    ├─ tokio::spawn → ExchangeRateService::start_sync_task()
│    ├─ scheduler::load_scheduler_config()
│    ├─ AffinityCache::default()
│    ├─ InMemoryBudget::new()
│    ├─ configure_rate_budget_from_db(...).await
│    ├─ read BILLING_STRICT_MODE
│    ├─ read REQUEST_LOG_STORAGE_POLICY
│    ├─ tokio::spawn → AIMD budget-update consumer
│    ├─ price_sync::start_price_sync_task(...)
│    ├─ tokio::spawn → RouterLog writer → RouterDatabase::insert_log()
│    ├─ tokio::spawn → RequestLog writer → RouterDatabase::insert_request_log()
│    ├─ ChannelHealthManager::new()
│    ├─ build internal_app routes
│    │    ├─ /console/internal/health
│    │    ├─ /console/internal/prices/sync
│    │    ├─ /console/internal/circuit-breaker/trip-all
│    │    └─ /console/internal/metrics
│    └─ build data-plane app
│         ├─ GET /v1/models → models_handler [REGISTER ONLY]
│         ├─ GET /api/v1/usage → usage_handler [REGISTER ONLY]
│         ├─ GET /api/v1/usage/models → usage_models_handler [REGISTER ONLY]
│         └─ fallback(proxy_handler) [REGISTER ONLY]
│
├─ IMPORTANT: 上述 Handler 在启动时只被注册；收到对应 HTTP 请求后才执行
│
▼
FILE: crates/service/crates/billing/src/cache.rs
│
├─ PriceCache::load(db)
│    ├─ PriceCache::empty()
│    └─ refresh(db)
│         └─ BillingPriceModel::list(...)
│
▼
FILE: crates/router/src/exchange_rate.rs
│
├─ ExchangeRateService::new()
├─ load_rates_from_db()
└─ start_sync_task()
     └─ tokio::spawn hourly loop
│
▼
FILE: crates/router/src/price_sync.rs
│
├─ start_price_sync_task(...)
│    └─ tokio::spawn
│         ├─ PriceSyncService::new()/with_config()
│         ├─ startup sync_all(false) unless SKIP_INITIAL_PRICE_SYNC
│         └─ LOOP select(periodic tick, force-sync receiver)
│
▼
FILE: crates/server/src/api/mod.rs
│
├─ api::routes(state)
│    ├─ auth::public_routes() [REGISTER]
│    ├─ auth::protected_routes() [REGISTER]
│    ├─ billing::routes() [REGISTER]
│    ├─ channel::routes() [REGISTER]
│    ├─ token::routes() [REGISTER]
│    ├─ log::routes() [REGISTER]

│    ├─ user::routes() [REGISTER]

│    ├─ openapi::routes() [REGISTER]

│
├─ liveview_router(db)

│    ├─ register /console and /console/*
│    ├─ register /favicon.ico
│    └─ register WS_PATH
│         └─ app::App is passed as future WebSocket-session callback [REGISTER ONLY]
│
▼
FILE: crates/server/src/lib.rs
│
├─ create_app() returns Unified Router
├─ SocketAddr::parse(host:port)
├─ TcpListener::bind(addr).await
├─ axum::serve(listener, app).await
│
└─ LONG-RUNNING STATE

     └─ RequestLog writer keeps running

END = server shutdown or fatal serve error
```

### E2E FLOW #26 — Windows tray thread

**树路径：** `Background Jobs / Async Side Effects → Desktop Background Work → Windows tray thread`  
**PR Impact：** `STATIC SOURCE-PATH MATCH`  
**本 Flow 命中的 changed files：** `crates/client/src/app.rs`, `crates/client/crates/client-tray/src/main.rs`, `crates/client/crates/client-shared/src/i18n.rs`

```text
START / TRIGGER
│
├─ Trigger source
│    ├─ startup spawn / request-time tokio::spawn / manager restoration / channel message
│    └─ task: Windows tray thread
│
├─ Spawn / registration
│    ├─ parent runtime creates async task/thread/loop
│    └─ DECISION: spawn/runtime handle available?
│         ├─ NO → task never starts; parent may log error
│         └─ YES → task owns/borrows required shared state
│
├─ Wait boundary
│    ├─ timer sleep / mpsc receive / polling interval / restored work item
│    └─ DECISION: trigger/event available?
│         ├─ NO → continue waiting
│         └─ YES → one iteration begins
│
├─ Input snapshot
│    ├─ read latest runtime/DB/request-derived state needed by job
│    └─ freeze iteration context
│
├─ Core job operation
│    ├─ execute Windows tray thread
│    └─ may call DB / HTTP / filesystem / runtime service depending on task
│
├─ Operation result
│    └─ DECISION: iteration succeeds?
│         ├─ NO
│         │    ├─ log/record failure
│         │    ├─ preserve parent request availability when task is fail-open
│         │    └─ decide retry on next event/interval
│         └─ YES
│              ├─ update in-memory state and/or persistent state
│              └─ emit success telemetry/log
│
├─ Cancellation / lifetime
│    └─ DECISION: parent runtime still alive AND task should continue?
│         ├─ YES → 返回等待边界
│         └─ NO → release task resources
│
│
├─ 源码函数展开（静态扫描确认）
```

### E2E FLOW #27 — Show-window poll loop

**树路径：** `Background Jobs / Async Side Effects → Desktop Background Work → Show-window poll loop`  
**PR Impact：** `STATIC SOURCE-PATH MATCH`  
**本 Flow 命中的 changed files：** `crates/client/src/app.rs`, `crates/client/crates/client-shared/src/i18n.rs`

```text
START / TRIGGER
│
├─ Trigger source
│    ├─ startup spawn / request-time tokio::spawn / manager restoration / channel message
│    └─ task: Show-window poll loop
│
├─ Spawn / registration
│    ├─ parent runtime creates async task/thread/loop
│    └─ DECISION: spawn/runtime handle available?
│         ├─ NO → task never starts; parent may log error
│         └─ YES → task owns/borrows required shared state
│
├─ Wait boundary
│    ├─ timer sleep / mpsc receive / polling interval / restored work item
│    └─ DECISION: trigger/event available?
│         ├─ NO → continue waiting
│         └─ YES → one iteration begins
│
├─ Input snapshot
│    ├─ read latest runtime/DB/request-derived state needed by job
│    └─ freeze iteration context
│
├─ Core job operation
│    ├─ execute Show-window poll loop
│    └─ may call DB / HTTP / filesystem / runtime service depending on task
│
├─ Operation result
│    └─ DECISION: iteration succeeds?
│         ├─ NO
│         │    ├─ log/record failure
│         │    ├─ preserve parent request availability when task is fail-open
│         │    └─ decide retry on next event/interval
│         └─ YES
│              ├─ update in-memory state and/or persistent state
│              └─ emit success telemetry/log
│
├─ Cancellation / lifetime
│    └─ DECISION: parent runtime still alive AND task should continue?
│         ├─ YES → 返回等待边界
│         └─ NO → release task resources
│
│
├─ 源码函数展开（静态扫描确认）
│    ├─ FILE: crates/client/src/app.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    ├─ App()

│    │    ├─ launch_gui_with_tray()
│    └─ FILE: crates/client/crates/client-shared/src/i18n.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    ├─ use_init_i18n()
│
├─ 规则：只展开能够解析到 BurnCloud 仓库内部真实函数定义的调用；第三方库调用保留在主 E2E 中，不伪造源码目标文件
│

▼
END / NEXT ITERATION
```

```text
FILE: crates/client/src/app.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│
├─ Dioxus Router match
│    └─ DECISION: Route enum/path matches?
│         ├─ NO → NotFound route/component
│         └─ YES → select mapped page component
│
├─ Route guards / context read
│    ├─ component can read Auth context
│    ├─ component can read Theme/i18n state
│    └─ DECISION: page requires authenticated state and context satisfies it?
│         ├─ NO → login/guard behavior as implemented by component/router
│         └─ YES → render page
│
▼
FILE: crates/client/src/pages/home.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│
└─ HomePage
│
├─ Component construction
│    ├─ initialize local signals/state
│    ├─ render initial VDOM
│    └─ register click/input/effect handlers
│
├─ Data boundary
│    └─ DECISION: component action/effect needs server data?
│         ├─ NO → remain local UI-only path
│         └─ YES → issue separate HTTP/API request
│              └─ that request is documented under HTTP / API, not hidden in this flow
│
├─ User-visible result
│    ├─ Dioxus reconciles VDOM
│    └─ page/render state becomes visible
│
├─ Event loop
│    └─ wait for next UI event/navigation/state update
│
│
├─ 源码函数展开（静态扫描确认）

│    │    ├─ App()
│    │    │    └─ CALL → use_init_i18n() @ crates/client/crates/client-shared/src/i18n.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    ├─ launch_gui_with_tray()
│    └─ FILE: crates/client/crates/client-shared/src/i18n.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    ├─ use_init_i18n()
│
├─ 规则：只展开能够解析到 BurnCloud 仓库内部真实函数定义的调用；第三方库调用保留在主 E2E 中，不伪造源码目标文件
```

### E2E FLOW #35 — /console/dashboard

**树路径：** `UI-only Actions → Console → /console/dashboard`  
**PR Impact：** `STATIC SOURCE-PATH MATCH`  
**本 Flow 命中的 changed files：** `crates/client/src/app.rs`, `crates/client/src/components/layout.rs`, `crates/client/crates/client-shared/src/components/layout.rs`, `crates/client/src/pages/dashboard.rs`, `crates/client/crates/client-dashboard/src/lib.rs`, `crates/client/crates/client-shared/src/i18n.rs`

```text
START
│
├─ Navigation input
│    ├─ browser/client route: /console/dashboard
│    ├─ current Auth context
│    ├─ current Theme/i18n/Toast context
│    └─ optional route params/query
│
▼
FILE: crates/client/src/app.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│
├─ Dioxus Router match
│    └─ DECISION: Route enum/path matches?
│         ├─ NO → NotFound route/component
│         └─ YES → select mapped page component
│
├─ Route guards / context read
│    ├─ component can read Auth context
│    ├─ component can read Theme/i18n state
│    └─ DECISION: page requires authenticated state and context satisfies it?
│         ├─ NO → login/guard behavior as implemented by component/router
│         └─ YES → render page
│
▼
FILE: crates/client/src/components/layout.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│
└─ Layout wraps console page / navigation / shared contexts
│
▼
FILE: crates/client/src/pages/dashboard.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│
└─ Dashboard re-export
│
▼
FILE: crates/client/crates/client-dashboard/src/lib.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│
└─ actual page component implementation / local effects
│
├─ Component construction
│    ├─ initialize local signals/state
│    ├─ render initial VDOM
│    └─ register click/input/effect handlers
│
├─ Data boundary
│    └─ DECISION: component action/effect needs server data?
│         ├─ NO → remain local UI-only path
│         └─ YES → issue separate HTTP/API request
│              └─ that request is documented under HTTP / API, not hidden in this flow
│
├─ User-visible result
│    ├─ Dioxus reconciles VDOM
│    └─ page/render state becomes visible
│
├─ Event loop
│    └─ wait for next UI event/navigation/state update
│
│
├─ 源码函数展开（静态扫描确认）
│    ├─ FILE: crates/client/src/app.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    ├─ App()
│    │    │    └─ CALL → use_init_i18n() @ crates/client/crates/client-shared/src/i18n.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    ├─ launch_gui_with_tray()
│    └─ FILE: crates/client/crates/client-shared/src/i18n.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    ├─ use_init_i18n()
│
├─ 规则：只展开能够解析到 BurnCloud 仓库内部真实函数定义的调用；第三方库调用保留在主 E2E 中，不伪造源码目标文件
│

▼
END / UI LOOP CONTINUES
```

**树路径：** `UI-only Actions → Console → /console/models`  
**PR Impact：** `STATIC SOURCE-PATH MATCH`

```text
START
│
├─ Navigation input
│    ├─ browser/client route: /console/models
│    ├─ current Auth context
│    ├─ current Theme/i18n/Toast context
│    └─ optional route params/query
│
▼
FILE: crates/client/src/app.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│
├─ Dioxus Router match
│    └─ DECISION: Route enum/path matches?
│         ├─ NO → NotFound route/component
│         └─ YES → select mapped page component
│
├─ Route guards / context read
│    ├─ component can read Auth context
│    ├─ component can read Theme/i18n state
│    └─ DECISION: page requires authenticated state and context satisfies it?
│         ├─ NO → login/guard behavior as implemented by component/router
│         └─ YES → render page
│
▼
FILE: crates/client/src/components/layout.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│
└─ Layout wraps console page / navigation / shared contexts
│
▼

│

│
└─ actual page component implementation / local effects
│
├─ Component construction
│    ├─ initialize local signals/state
│    ├─ render initial VDOM
│    └─ register click/input/effect handlers
│
├─ Data boundary
│    └─ DECISION: component action/effect needs server data?
│         ├─ NO → remain local UI-only path
│         └─ YES → issue separate HTTP/API request
│              └─ that request is documented under HTTP / API, not hidden in this flow
│
├─ User-visible result
│    ├─ Dioxus reconciles VDOM
│    └─ page/render state becomes visible
│
├─ Event loop
│    └─ wait for next UI event/navigation/state update
│
│
├─ 源码函数展开（静态扫描确认）
│    ├─ FILE: crates/client/src/app.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    ├─ App()

│    └─ FILE: crates/client/crates/client-shared/src/i18n.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    ├─ use_init_i18n()
│
├─ 规则：只展开能够解析到 BurnCloud 仓库内部真实函数定义的调用；第三方库调用保留在主 E2E 中，不伪造源码目标文件
```

```text
│    └─ optional route params/query
│
▼
FILE: crates/client/src/app.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│
├─ Dioxus Router match
│    └─ DECISION: Route enum/path matches?
│         ├─ NO → NotFound route/component
│         └─ YES → select mapped page component
│
├─ Route guards / context read
│    ├─ component can read Auth context
│    ├─ component can read Theme/i18n state
│    └─ DECISION: page requires authenticated state and context satisfies it?
│         ├─ NO → login/guard behavior as implemented by component/router
│         └─ YES → render page
│
▼
FILE: crates/client/src/components/layout.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│
└─ Layout wraps console page / navigation / shared contexts
│
▼
FILE: crates/client/src/pages/billing.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│
└─ FinancePage re-export
│
▼
FILE: crates/client/crates/client-finance/src/lib.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│
└─ actual page component implementation / local effects
│
├─ Component construction
│    ├─ initialize local signals/state
│    ├─ render initial VDOM
│    └─ register click/input/effect handlers
│
├─ Data boundary
│    └─ DECISION: component action/effect needs server data?
│         ├─ NO → remain local UI-only path
│         └─ YES → issue separate HTTP/API request
│              └─ that request is documented under HTTP / API, not hidden in this flow
│
├─ User-visible result
│    ├─ Dioxus reconciles VDOM
│    └─ page/render state becomes visible
│
├─ Event loop
│    └─ wait for next UI event/navigation/state update
│
│
├─ 源码函数展开（静态扫描确认）
│    ├─ FILE: crates/client/src/app.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    ├─ App()
│    │    │    └─ CALL → use_init_i18n() @ crates/client/crates/client-shared/src/i18n.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    ├─ launch_gui_with_tray()
│    └─ FILE: crates/client/crates/client-shared/src/i18n.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    ├─ use_init_i18n()
│
├─ 规则：只展开能够解析到 BurnCloud 仓库内部真实函数定义的调用；第三方库调用保留在主 E2E 中，不伪造源码目标文件
│

▼
END / UI LOOP CONTINUES
```

**树路径：** `UI-only Actions → Console → /console/:..segments → NotFound`  
**PR Impact：** `STATIC SOURCE-PATH MATCH`  
**本 Flow 命中的 changed files：** `crates/client/src/app.rs`, `crates/client/src/components/layout.rs`, `crates/client/crates/client-shared/src/components/layout.rs`, `crates/client/src/pages/not_found.rs`, `crates/client/crates/client-shared/src/i18n.rs`

```text
START
│
├─ Navigation input
│    ├─ browser/client route: /console/:..segments → NotFound
│    ├─ current Auth context
│    ├─ current Theme/i18n/Toast context
│    └─ optional route params/query
│
▼
FILE: crates/client/src/app.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│
├─ Dioxus Router match
│    └─ DECISION: Route enum/path matches?
│         ├─ NO → NotFound route/component
│         └─ YES → select mapped page component
│
├─ Route guards / context read
│    ├─ component can read Auth context
│    ├─ component can read Theme/i18n state
│    └─ DECISION: page requires authenticated state and context satisfies it?
│         ├─ NO → login/guard behavior as implemented by component/router
│         └─ YES → render page
│
▼
FILE: crates/client/src/components/layout.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│
└─ Layout wraps console page / navigation / shared contexts
│
▼
FILE: crates/client/src/pages/not_found.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│
└─ NotFoundPage
│
├─ Component construction
│    ├─ initialize local signals/state
│    ├─ render initial VDOM
│    └─ register click/input/effect handlers
│
├─ Data boundary
│    └─ DECISION: component action/effect needs server data?
│         ├─ NO → remain local UI-only path
│         └─ YES → issue separate HTTP/API request
│              └─ that request is documented under HTTP / API, not hidden in this flow
│
├─ User-visible result
│    ├─ Dioxus reconciles VDOM
│    └─ page/render state becomes visible
│
├─ Event loop
│    └─ wait for next UI event/navigation/state update
│
│
├─ 源码函数展开（静态扫描确认）
│    ├─ FILE: crates/client/src/app.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    ├─ App()
│    │    │    └─ CALL → use_init_i18n() @ crates/client/crates/client-shared/src/i18n.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    ├─ launch_gui_with_tray()
│    └─ FILE: crates/client/crates/client-shared/src/i18n.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    ├─ use_init_i18n()
│
├─ 规则：只展开能够解析到 BurnCloud 仓库内部真实函数定义的调用；第三方库调用保留在主 E2E 中，不伪造源码目标文件
│

▼
END / UI LOOP CONTINUES
```

```text
│    └─ DECISION: Route enum/path matches?
│         ├─ NO → NotFound route/component
│         └─ YES → select mapped page component
│
├─ Route guards / context read
│    ├─ component can read Auth context
│    ├─ component can read Theme/i18n state
│    └─ DECISION: page requires authenticated state and context satisfies it?
│         ├─ NO → login/guard behavior as implemented by component/router
│         └─ YES → render page
│
▼
FILE: crates/client/src/pages/e2e_preview.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│
└─ selected Preview*Page component renders deterministic preview state
│
├─ Component construction
│    ├─ initialize local signals/state
│    ├─ render initial VDOM
│    └─ register click/input/effect handlers
│
├─ Data boundary
│    └─ DECISION: component action/effect needs server data?
│         ├─ NO → remain local UI-only path
│         └─ YES → issue separate HTTP/API request
│              └─ that request is documented under HTTP / API, not hidden in this flow
│
├─ User-visible result
│    ├─ Dioxus reconciles VDOM
│    └─ page/render state becomes visible
│
├─ Event loop
│    └─ wait for next UI event/navigation/state update
│
│
├─ 源码函数展开（静态扫描确认）
│    ├─ FILE: crates/client/src/app.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    ├─ App()
│    │    │    └─ CALL → use_init_i18n() @ crates/client/crates/client-shared/src/i18n.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    ├─ launch_gui_with_tray()
│    └─ FILE: crates/client/crates/client-shared/src/i18n.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    ├─ use_init_i18n()
│
├─ 规则：只展开能够解析到 BurnCloud 仓库内部真实函数定义的调用；第三方库调用保留在主 E2E 中，不伪造源码目标文件
│

▼
END / UI LOOP CONTINUES
```

**树路径：** `UI-only Actions → Debug / e2e-preview → /preview/console/playground`  
**PR Impact：** `STATIC SOURCE-PATH MATCH`  
**本 Flow 命中的 changed files：** `crates/client/src/app.rs`, `crates/client/src/pages/e2e_preview.rs`, `crates/client/crates/client-shared/src/i18n.rs`

```text
START
│
├─ Navigation input
│    ├─ browser/client route: /preview/console/playground
│    ├─ current Auth context
│    ├─ current Theme/i18n/Toast context
│    └─ optional route params/query
│
▼
FILE: crates/client/src/app.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│
├─ Dioxus Router match
│    └─ DECISION: Route enum/path matches?
│         ├─ NO → NotFound route/component
│         └─ YES → select mapped page component
│
├─ Route guards / context read
│    ├─ component can read Auth context
│    ├─ component can read Theme/i18n state
│    └─ DECISION: page requires authenticated state and context satisfies it?
│         ├─ NO → login/guard behavior as implemented by component/router
│         └─ YES → render page
│
▼
FILE: crates/client/src/pages/e2e_preview.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│
└─ selected Preview*Page component renders deterministic preview state
│
├─ Component construction
│    ├─ initialize local signals/state
│    ├─ render initial VDOM
│    └─ register click/input/effect handlers
│
├─ Data boundary
│    └─ DECISION: component action/effect needs server data?
│         ├─ NO → remain local UI-only path
│         └─ YES → issue separate HTTP/API request
│              └─ that request is documented under HTTP / API, not hidden in this flow
│
├─ User-visible result
│    ├─ Dioxus reconciles VDOM
│    └─ page/render state becomes visible
│
├─ Event loop
│    └─ wait for next UI event/navigation/state update
│
│
├─ 源码函数展开（静态扫描确认）
│    ├─ FILE: crates/client/src/app.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    ├─ App()
│    │    │    └─ CALL → use_init_i18n() @ crates/client/crates/client-shared/src/i18n.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    ├─ launch_gui_with_tray()
│    └─ FILE: crates/client/crates/client-shared/src/i18n.rs  [PR CHANGED]
│    │    ├─ use_init_i18n()
│
├─ 规则：只展开能够解析到 BurnCloud 仓库内部真实函数定义的调用；第三方库调用保留在主 E2E 中，不伪造源码目标文件
│

▼
END / UI LOOP CONTINUES
```
